c.y)<4))continue;chosen.push(c);}}
    const contourToleranceM16166=5.0;
    function contourAudit16166(candidate){
      const values=[],coords=candidate&&candidate.segment||[],stride=Math.max(1,Math.floor(coords.length/28));
      for(let i=0;i<coords.length;i+=stride){
        const g=llGrid(hy,coords[i]),x=Math.max(0,Math.min(hy.w-1,Math.round(g.x))),y=Math.max(0,Math.min(hy.h-1,Math.round(g.y))),v=Number(hy.elev[y*hy.w+x]);
        if(Number.isFinite(v))values.push(v);
      }
      if(coords.length&&values.length<2){
        for(const p of [coords[0],coords[coords.length-1]]){const g=llGrid(hy,p),x=Math.max(0,Math.min(hy.w-1,Math.round(g.x))),y=Math.max(0,Math.min(hy.h-1,Math.round(g.y))),v=Number(hy.elev[y*hy.w+x]);if(Number.isFinite(v))values.push(v);}
      }
      const min=values.length?Math.min(...values):Infinity,max=values.length?Math.max(...values):-Infinity;
      const relief=values.length?max-min:Infinity,endDrop=values.length>1?Math.abs(values[values.length-1]-values[0]):Infinity;
      return {reliefM:relief,endDropM:endDrop,valid:Number.isFinite(relief)&&relief<=contourToleranceM16166&&endDrop<=contourToleranceM16166};
    }
    const auditedCandidates=chosen.map(c=>Object.assign(c,{contourAudit16166:contourAudit16166(c)}));
    /* BUILD-CHECK-16167: the 5 m longitudinal gate is a focus-screening gate.
       Applying that tolerance to Vermont's coarse regional DEM rejected every
       regional corridor because nearest-cell sampling can vary by more than 5 m.
       Regional corridors remain indicative and carry the audit values, but are
       not silently erased by a property/focus-scale tolerance. */
    const acceptedChosen=focusMode?auditedCandidates.filter(c=>c.contourAudit16166.valid):auditedCandidates;
    const contourAudit={
      build:'EARTHLINE 16167',tier:focusMode?'focus':'regional',enforced:!!focusMode,
      evaluated:chosen.length,accepted:acceptedChosen.length,rejected:chosen.length-acceptedChosen.length,
      toleranceM:contourToleranceM16166,
      maxAcceptedReliefM:acceptedChosen.length?Math.max(...acceptedChosen.map(c=>Number(c.contourAudit16166.reliefM)||0)):0,
      rule:focusMode?'focus corridors must remain within 5 m longitudinal relief and 5 m end-to-end elevation change':'regional corridors retain modeled contour geometry; the 5 m focus tolerance is recorded but not enforced at regional DEM scale',

    const gradeACount=Math.max(3,Math.ceil(total*.16));
    const gradeBEnd=Math.max(gradeACount+4,Math.ceil(total*.48));
    for(let index=0;index<acceptedChosen.length;index++){
      const c=acceptedChosen[index],rank=index+1,score=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Math.round(c.score*100)));
      const grade=index<gradeACount?'A':index<gradeBEnd?'B':'C';
      const gradeLabel=grade==='A'?'BEST':grade==='B'?'STRONG':'GENERAL';
      const slopeFit=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Math.round(100-Math.abs(c.slope-3.4)*9)));
      const relief=Number(c.contourAudit16166.reliefM),drop=Number(c.contourAudit16166.endDropM);
      features.push({type:'Feature',properties:{
        feature_type:'swale-opportunity',
        grade,rank,score,

        slope_pct:Number(c.slope.toFixed(1)),
        slope_fit_score:slopeFit,
        confidence:c.confidence,
        contour_valid:focusMode?true:null,
        contour_validation_class:focusMode?'focus-screening-validated':'regional-screening-not-enforced',
        contour_tolerance_m:contourToleranceM16166,
        longitudinal_relief_m:Number.isFinite(relief)?Number(relief.toFixed(2)):null,
        end_to_end_drop_m:Number.isFinite(drop)?Number(drop.toFixed(2)):null,
        label:grade+rank+' — '+gradeLabel+' '+(focusMode?'FOCUS':'REGIONAL')+' BIOSWALE OPPORTUNITY',
        screening:focusMode?'Earthline focus corridor derived from a modeled contour; longitudinal relief and end-to-end elevation change are each limited to 5 m.     }

      build:'EARTHLINE 16167',tier:focusMode?'focus':'regional',candidates:candidatesBeforeJurisdiction16539,
      preferredJurisdictionEligibleCandidates:preferredEligibleCount16539,

      chosenBeforeTierGate:chosen.length,publishedFeatures:features.length,
      focusContourGateEnforced:!!focusMode,focusContourGateRejected:chosen.length-acceptedChosen.length,
      nullReason:features.length?'corridors published':(candidates.length?'all candidates rejected by the active tier gate':'no terrain-derived contour segments met slope, flow and spacing criteria'),

  function jsonp(url,params,ms=22000){
    return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
      const cb='__earthlineJsonp15970_'+Date.now()+'_'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2),script=document.createElement('script');
      const cleanup=()=>{try{delete window[cb];}catch(_){window[cb]=undefined;}script.remove();clearTimeout(timer);};
      const timer=setTimeout(()=>{cleanup();reject(new Error('JSONP request timed out'));},ms);
      window[cb]=data=>{cleanup();resolve(data);};
      const p=new URLSearchParams(params||{});p.set('callback',cb);p.set('f','json');
      script.async=true;script.onerror=()=>{cleanup();reject(new Error('JSONP request failed'));};
      script.src=url+'?'+p.toString();document.head.appendChild(script);
    });
  }
  function esriPolygonToGeoJSON(feature){
    const g=feature&&feature.geometry,attrs=feature&&feature.attributes||{};if(!g||!Array.isArray(g.rings))return null;
    const polys=g.rings.filter(r=>Array.isArray(r)&&r.length>=4).map(r=>[r.map(p=>[Number(p[0]),Number(p[1])])]);
    if(!polys.length)return null;
    return {type:'Feature',properties:attrs,geometry:polys.length===1?{type:'Polygon',coordinates:polys[0]}:{type:'MultiPolygon',coordinates:polys}};
  }
  function esriSetToGeoJSON(data){
    const features=(data&&data.features||[]).map(esriPolygonToGeoJSON).filter(Boolean);
    return {type:'FeatureCollection',features};
  }
  /* EARTHLINE 16565 contract restored — optional HydroBASINS/USGS context for a

  function earthlineOptionalContextFeatureContained16565(feature,boundary16565){
    const g16565=boundary16565&&(boundary16565.prepared||boundary16565.geometry);
    const geom16565=feature&&feature.geometry;

  function earthlineBasinDisplayLandSplitMantra38(geoMantra38,runTokenMantra38){
    const validityMantra38=window.EARTHLINE_LAND_VALIDITY_16584||null,
          waterPartsMantra38=Array.isArray(validityMantra38&&validityMantra38.waterParts)?validityMantra38.waterParts:[];
    if(!geoMantra38||!Array.isArray(geoMantra38.features)||!waterPartsMantra38.length)return geoMantra38;
    const finiteMantra38=p=>Array.isArray(p)&&Number.isFinite(Number(p[0]))&&Number.isFinite(Number(p[1]));
    const inWaterMantra38=p=>{
      if(!finiteMantra38(p))return true;
      const x=Number(p[0]),y=Number(p[1]);
      for(const part of waterPartsMantra38){
        if(!part||!part.bbox||!part.rings)continue;
        if(x<part.bbox[0]||x>part.bbox[2]||y<part.bbox[1]||y>part.bbox[3])continue;
        if(earthlinePointInPolygon16584(x,y,part.rings))return true;
      }
      return false;
    };
    const sameMantra38=(a,b)=>finiteMantra38(a)&&finiteMantra38(b)&&Math.abs(Number(a[0])-Number(b[0]))<1e-10&&Math.abs(Number(a[1])-Number(b[1]))<1e-10;
    const clipRingMantra38=ring=>{
      const segments=[];let current=[];
      for(const raw of (Array.isArray(ring)?ring:[])){
        if(!finiteMantra38(raw)){if(current.length>1)segments.push(current);current=[];continue;}
        const p=[Number(raw[0]),Number(raw[1])];
        if(inWaterMantra38(p)){if(current.length>1)segments.push(current);current=[];continue;}
        current.push(p);
      }
      if(current.length>1)segments.push(current);
      if(segments.length>1&&sameMantra38(segments[0][0],segments[segments.length-1][segments[segments.length-1].length-1])){
        const last=segments.pop(),first=segments.shift();segments.unshift(last.concat(first.slice(1)));
      }
      return segments;
    };
    const out=[];let polygonFeatures=0,lineFeatures=0,lineParts=0;
    for(const feature of geoMantra38.features){
      const g=feature&&feature.geometry,props=Object.assign({},feature&&feature.properties||{});
      if(!g||(g.type!=='Polygon'&&g.type!=='MultiPolygon')){out.push(feature);continue;}
      polygonFeatures++;
      out.push({type:'Feature',properties:Object.assign({},props,{earthline_context_geometry:'fill'}),geometry:g});
      const polys=g.type==='Polygon'?[g.coordinates]:(g.coordinates||[]),lines=[];
      for(const poly of polys)for(const ring of (poly||[]))for(const seg of clipRingMantra38(ring)){if(seg.length>1){lines.push(seg);lineParts++;}}
      if(lines.length){
        out.push({type:'Feature',properties:Object.assign({},props,{earthline_context_geometry:'line'}),geometry:{type:'MultiLineString',coordinates:lines}});
        lineFeatures++;
      }
    }
    window.EARTHLINE_BASIN_WATER_DISPLAY_AUDIT_MANTRA38={runToken:runTokenMantra38||null,inputFeatures:geoMantra38.features.length,polygonFeatures,lineFeatures,lineParts,waterParts:waterPartsMantra38.length,rule:'basin fill preserved; basin boundary display withheld at mapped inland-water vertices',at:new Date().toISOString()};
    return {type:'FeatureCollection',features:out};
  }

  function normalizeUSGSKarst(geo){

      p.boundary_class='national/regional guidance — not a parcel boundary';

    });

  }

  async function loadAquifers(b,runToken,optionalBoundary16565=null){

  async function settleRenderedScientificLayers(m,expectedAquiferCount,ms=6500,runToken){
    if(runToken&&!isCurrentRun(runToken))return {ok:false,superseded:true};
    const started=Date.now();let idle=false;
    try{m.triggerRepaint&&m.triggerRepaint();}catch(_){}
    try{await Promise.race([new Promise(resolve=>m.once('idle',()=>{idle=true;resolve();})),wait(ms)]);}catch(_){}
    const sourceState={};
    for(const id of [IDS.contours,IDS.flows,IDS.swales,IDS.aquifer]){
      try{sourceState[id]=!!(m.getSource&&m.getSource(id))&&(!m.isSourceLoaded||m.isSourceLoaded(id));}catch(_){sourceState[id]=false;}
    }
    const layersVisible={};
    for(const id of [IDS.contourLine,IDS.flowLine,IDS.swaleLine,IDS.aquiferFill]){
      try{layersVisible[id]=!!m.getLayer(id)&&m.getLayoutProperty(id,'visibility')!=='none';}catch(_){layersVisible[id]=false;}
    }

  function normalizeKnownLocationKey(v){return clean(v).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,' ').trim();}

    }catch(_){return null;}
  }

  function jsonp(url,params,ms=22000){
    return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
      const cb='__earthlineJsonp15970_'+Date.now()+'_'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2),script=document.createElement('script');
      const cleanup=()=>{try{delete window[cb];}catch(_){window[cb]=undefined;}script.remove();clearTimeout(timer);};
      const timer=setTimeout(()=>{cleanup();reject(new Error('JSONP request timed out'));},ms);
      window[cb]=data=>{cleanup();resolve(data);};
      const p=new URLSearchParams(params||{});p.set('callback',cb);p.set('f','json');
      script.async=true;script.onerror=()=>{cleanup();reject(new Error('JSONP request failed'));};
      script.src=url+'?'+p.toString();document.head.appendChild(script);
    });
  }
  function esriPolygonToGeoJSON(feature){
    const g=feature&&feature.geometry,attrs=feature&&feature.attributes||{};if(!g||!Array.isArray(g.rings))return null;
    const polys=g.rings.filter(r=>Array.isArray(r)&&r.length>=4).map(r=>[r.map(p=>[Number(p[0]),Number(p[1])])]);
    if(!polys.length)return null;
    return {type:'Feature',properties:attrs,geometry:polys.length===1?{type:'Polygon',coordinates:polys[0]}:{type:'MultiPolygon',coordinates:polys}};
  }
  function esriSetToGeoJSON(data){
    const features=(data&&data.features||[]).map(esriPolygonToGeoJSON).filter(Boolean);
    return {type:'FeatureCollection',features};
  }
  /* EARTHLINE 16565 contract restored — optional HydroBASINS/USGS context for a
     U.S. state is published only when the complete source polygon is inside the
     already-resolved Census state boundary. Cross-boundary polygons are withheld,
     never truncated. This is presentation/context containment only. */
  function earthlineOptionalContextFeatureContained16565(feature,boundary16565){
    const g16565=boundary16565&&(boundary16565.prepared||boundary16565.geometry);
    const geom16565=feature&&feature.geometry;
    if(!g16565||!geom16565)return false;
    const polys16565=geom16565.type==='Polygon'?[geom16565.coordinates||[]]:geom16565.type==='MultiPolygon'?(geom16565.coordinates||[]):[];
    if(!polys16565.length)return false;
    for(const rings16565 of polys16565){
      for(const ring16565 of (rings16565||[])){
        const pts16565=(ring16565||[]).filter(earthlineFinitePoint16539);
        if(pts16565.length<4)return false;
        for(let i16565=0;i16565<pts16565.length;i16565++){
          const a16565=pts16565[i16565],b16565=pts16565[(i16565+1)%pts16565.length];
          if(!earthlinePointInJurisdiction16539(a16565,g16565))return false;
          const steps16565=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(Math.max(Math.abs(Number(b16565[0])-Number(a16565[0])),Math.abs(Number(b16565[1])-Number(a16565[1])))/.01));
          for(let k16565=1;k16565<steps16565;k16565++)if(!earthlinePointInJurisdiction16539(earthlineInterpolate16539(a16565,b16565,k16565/steps16565),g16565))return false;
        }
      }
    }
    return true;
  }
  function earthlineBasinDisplayLandSplitMantra38(geoMantra38,runTokenMantra38){
    const validityMantra38=window.EARTHLINE_LAND_VALIDITY_16584||null,
          waterPartsMantra38=Array.isArray(validityMantra38&&validityMantra38.waterParts)?validityMantra38.waterParts:[];
    if(!geoMantra38||!Array.isArray(geoMantra38.features)||!waterPartsMantra38.length)return geoMantra38;
    const finiteMantra38=p=>Array.isArray(p)&&Number.isFinite(Number(p[0]))&&Number.isFinite(Number(p[1]));
    const inWaterMantra38=p=>{
      if(!finiteMantra38(p))return true;
      const x=Number(p[0]),y=Number(p[1]);
      for(const part of waterPartsMantra38){
        if(!part||!part.bbox||!part.rings)continue;
        if(x<part.bbox[0]||x>part.bbox[2]||y<part.bbox[1]||y>part.bbox[3])continue;
        if(earthlinePointInPolygon16584(x,y,part.rings))return true;
      }
      return false;
    };
    const sameMantra38=(a,b)=>finiteMantra38(a)&&finiteMantra38(b)&&Math.abs(Number(a[0])-Number(b[0]))<1e-10&&Math.abs(Number(a[1])-Number(b[1]))<1e-10;
    const clipRingMantra38=ring=>{
      const segments=[];let current=[];
      for(const raw of (Array.isArray(ring)?ring:[])){
        if(!finiteMantra38(raw)){if(current.length>1)segments.push(current);current=[];continue;}
        const p=[Number(raw[0]),Number(raw[1])];
        if(inWaterMantra38(p)){if(current.length>1)segments.push(current);current=[];continue;}
        current.push(p);
      }
      if(current.length>1)segments.push(current);
      if(segments.length>1&&sameMantra38(segments[0][0],segments[segments.length-1][segments[segments.length-1].length-1])){
        const last=segments.pop(),first=segments.shift();segments.unshift(last.concat(first.slice(1)));
      }
      return segments;
    };
    const out=[];let polygonFeatures=0,lineFeatures=0,lineParts=0;
    for(const feature of geoMantra38.features){
      const g=feature&&feature.geometry,props=Object.assign({},feature&&feature.properties||{});
      if(!g||(g.type!=='Polygon'&&g.type!=='MultiPolygon')){out.push(feature);continue;}
      polygonFeatures++;
      out.push({type:'Feature',properties:Object.assign({},props,{earthline_context_geometry:'fill'}),geometry:g});
      const polys=g.type==='Polygon'?[g.coordinates]:(g.coordinates||[]),lines=[];
      for(const poly of polys)for(const ring of (poly||[]))for(const seg of clipRingMantra38(ring)){if(seg.length>1){lines.push(seg);lineParts++;}}
      if(lines.length){
        out.push({type:'Feature',properties:Object.assign({},props,{earthline_context_geometry:'line'}),geometry:{type:'MultiLineString',coordinates:lines}});
        lineFeatures++;
      }
    }
    window.EARTHLINE_BASIN_WATER_DISPLAY_AUDIT_MANTRA38={runToken:runTokenMantra38||null,inputFeatures:geoMantra38.features.length,polygonFeatures,lineFeatures,lineParts,waterParts:waterPartsMantra38.length,rule:'basin fill preserved; basin boundary display withheld at mapped inland-water vertices',at:new Date().toISOString()};
    return {type:'FeatureCollection',features:out};
  }

  function normalizeUSGSKarst(geo){

      const description=clean(p.USGS_Desc1||p.DESCRIPT||p.Type_Desc||p.AGI_Desc||'Mapped carbonate/karst groundwater terrain');
      p.name=(kind?kind+' karst groundwater context':'Karst groundwater context');
      p.source='USGS Open-File Report 2004-1352';
      p.boundary_class='national/regional guidance — not a parcel boundary';
      p.description=description;
      return {type:'Feature',properties:p,geometry:f.geometry};
    });
    return {type:'FeatureCollection',features};
  }

  function cameraCoverage(m,b){
    try{
      const cam=m&&m.getBounds&&m.getBounds();
      if(!cam)return 0;
      const cb=[cam.getWest(),cam.getSouth(),cam.getEast(),cam.getNorth()];
      const area=(b[2]-b[0])*(b[3]-b[1]);
      const overlap=Math.max(0,Math.min(b[2],cb[2])-Math.max(b[0],cb[0]))*Math.max(0,Math.min(b[3],cb[3])-Math.max(b[1],cb[1]));
      return area>0?overlap/area:0;
    }catch(_){return 0;}
  }
  async function settleRegionalCamera(m,b,runToken){

  function earthlinePointOnSegment16539(p,a,b){

  }

  function cameraCoverage(m,b){
    try{
      const cam=m&&m.getBounds&&m.getBounds();
      if(!cam)return 0;
      const cb=[cam.getWest(),cam.getSouth(),cam.getEast(),cam.getNorth()];
      const area=(b[2]-b[0])*(b[3]-b[1]);
      const overlap=Math.max(0,Math.min(b[2],cb[2])-Math.max(b[0],cb[0]))*Math.max(0,Math.min(b[3],cb[3])-Math.max(b[1],cb[1]));
      return area>0?overlap/area:0;
    }catch(_){return 0;}
  }

  function cameraCoverage(m,b){
    try{
      const cam=m&&m.getBounds&&m.getBounds();
      if(!cam)return 0;

      return area>0?overlap/area:0;
    }catch(_){return 0;}
  }

  }

  function jsonp(url,params,ms=22000){

      const timer=setTimeout(()=>{cleanup();reject(new Error('JSONP request timed out'));},ms);

  function esriPolygonToGeoJSON(feature){

  }

  function earthlineBasinDisplayLandSplitMantra38(geoMantra38,runTokenMantra38){

  function earthlineClipLine16539(coords,g){
    const raw=(coords||[]).filter(earthlineFinitePoint16539);if(raw.length<2)return [];
    const pts=[raw[0]];for(let i=1;i<raw.length;i++){const a=raw[i-1],b=raw[i],steps=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(Math.max(Math.abs(Number(b[0])-Number(a[0])),Math.abs(Number(b[1])-Number(a[1])))/.01));for(let k=1;k<=steps;k++)pts.push(earthlineInterpolate16539(a,b,k/steps));}
    const runs=[];let run=[];let prev=pts[0],prevIn=earthlinePointInJurisdiction16539(prev,g);if(prevIn)run=[prev];
    for(let i=1;i<pts.length;i++){const cur=pts[i],curIn=earthlinePointInJurisdiction16539(cur,g);if(prevIn&&curIn){if(!run.length)run=[prev];run.push(cur)}else if(prevIn&&!curIn){const x=earthlineBoundaryPoint16539(prev,cur,g,true);if(!run.length)run=[prev];run.push(x);if(run.length>=2)runs.push(run);run=[]}else if(!prevIn&&curIn){const x=earthlineBoundaryPoint16539(prev,cur,g,false);run=[x,cur]}prev=cur;prevIn=curIn;}
    if(run.length>=2)runs.push(run);return runs;
  }
  async function earthlineClipFeatureCollection16539(fc,g){

        }else{

        }

    }

  }

  function earthlineGeometryBBox16584(g16584){
    let minX16584=Infinity,minY16584=Infinity,maxX16584=-Infinity,maxY16584=-Infinity;
    const walk16584=v16584=>{
      if(!Array.isArray(v16584))return;
      if(v16584.length>=2&&Number.isFinite(Number(v16584[0]))&&Number.isFinite(Number(v16584[1]))){
        const x16584=Number(v16584[0]),y16584=Number(v16584[1]);
        minX16584=Math.min(minX16584,x16584);minY16584=Math.min(minY16584,y16584);
        maxX16584=Math.max(maxX16584,x16584);maxY16584=Math.max(maxY16584,y16584);return;
      }
      for(const c16584 of v16584)walk16584(c16584);
    };
    walk16584(g16584&&g16584.coordinates);
    return [minX16584,minY16584,maxX16584,maxY16584].every(Number.isFinite)
      ?[minX16584,minY16584,maxX16584,maxY16584]:null;
  }

  function earthlineBBoxIntersects16584(a16584,b16584){

  }
  function earthlinePolygonParts16584(g16584){

    if(g16584.type==="MultiPolygon")return Array.isArray(g16584.coordinates)?g16584.coordinates:[];

  }
  function earthlineRingBBox16584(ring16584){
    let minX16584=Infinity,minY16584=Infinity,maxX16584=-Infinity,maxY16584=-Infinity;
    for(const p16584 of (ring16584||[])){
      const x16584=Number(p16584&&p16584[0]),y16584=Number(p16584&&p16584[1]);

      minX16584=Math.min(minX16584,x16584);minY16584=Math.min(minY16584,y16584);
      maxX16584=Math.max(maxX16584,x16584);maxY16584=Math.max(maxY16584,y16584);
    }
    return [minX16584,minY16584,maxX16584,maxY16584].every(Number.isFinite)
      ?[minX16584,minY16584,maxX16584,maxY16584]:null;
  }
  function earthlinePointInRing16584(x16584,y16584,ring16584){
    if(!Array.isArray(ring16584)||ring16584.length<3)return false;
    let inside16584=false;
    for(let i16584=0,j16584=ring16584.length-1;i16584<ring16584.length;j16584=i16584++){
      const a16584=ring16584[i16584]||[],b16584=ring16584[j16584]||[];
      const xi16584=Number(a16584[0]),yi16584=Number(a16584[1]),
            xj16584=Number(b16584[0]),yj16584=Number(b16584[1]);
      if(![xi16584,yi16584,xj16584,yj16584].every(Number.isFinite))continue;
      if(((yi16584>y16584)!==(yj16584>y16584))&&
         (x16584<(xj16584-xi16584)*(y16584-yi16584)/((yj16584-yi16584)||1e-15)+xi16584))
        inside16584=!inside16584;
    }
    return inside16584;
  }
  function earthlinePointInPolygon16584(x16584,y16584,rings16584){
    if(!Array.isArray(rings16584)||!rings16584.length||

    for(let i16584=1;i16584<rings16584.length;i16584++)
      if(earthlinePointInRing16584(x16584,y16584,rings16584[i16584]))return false;
    return true;
  }
  function earthlineExtractAreaEvidence16584(fc16584,bounds16584){
    const features16584=[],parts16584=[];
    for(const f16584 of (fc16584&&fc16584.features||[])){

        const rb16584=earthlineRingBBox16584(rings16584&&rings16584[0]);
        if(rb16584&&earthlineBBoxIntersects16584(rb16584,bounds16584)){
          parts16584.push({rings:rings16584,bbox:rb16584,properties:f16584.properties||{}});
          kept16584++;
        }
      }
      if(kept16584)features16584.push(f16584);
    }
    return {features:features16584,parts:parts16584};
  }

  function earthlineLandValidityMask16584(dem16584,product16584){

  function earthlineClipLine16539(coords,g){
    const raw=(coords||[]).filter(earthlineFinitePoint16539);if(raw.length<2)return [];
    const pts=[raw[0]];for(let i=1;i<raw.length;i++){const a=raw[i-1],b=raw[i],steps=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(Math.max(Math.abs(Number(b[0])-Number(a[0])),Math.abs(Number(b[1])-Number(a[1])))/.01));for(let k=1;k<=steps;k++)pts.push(earthlineInterpolate16539(a,b,k/steps));}
    const runs=[];let run=[];let prev=pts[0],prevIn=earthlinePointInJurisdiction16539(prev,g);if(prevIn)run=[prev];
    for(let i=1;i<pts.length;i++){const cur=pts[i],curIn=earthlinePointInJurisdiction16539(cur,g);if(prevIn&&curIn){if(!run.length)run=[prev];run.push(cur)}else if(prevIn&&!curIn){const x=earthlineBoundaryPoint16539(prev,cur,g,true);if(!run.length)run=[prev];run.push(x);if(run.length>=2)runs.push(run);run=[]}else if(!prevIn&&curIn){const x=earthlineBoundaryPoint16539(prev,cur,g,false);run=[x,cur]}prev=cur;prevIn=curIn;}
    if(run.length>=2)runs.push(run);return runs;
  }
  async function earthlineClipFeatureCollection16539(fc,g){

        }else{

        }

  }

  function earthlineGeometryBBox16584(g16584){
    let minX16584=Infinity,minY16584=Infinity,maxX16584=-Infinity,maxY16584=-Infinity;
    const walk16584=v16584=>{
      if(!Array.isArray(v16584))return;
      if(v16584.length>=2&&Number.isFinite(Number(v16584[0]))&&Number.isFinite(Number(v16584[1]))){
        const x16584=Number(v16584[0]),y16584=Number(v16584[1]);
        minX16584=Math.min(minX16584,x16584);minY16584=Math.min(minY16584,y16584);
        maxX16584=Math.max(maxX16584,x16584);maxY16584=Math.max(maxY16584,y16584);return;
      }
      for(const c16584 of v16584)walk16584(c16584);
    };
    walk16584(g16584&&g16584.coordinates);
    return [minX16584,minY16584,maxX16584,maxY16584].every(Number.isFinite)
      ?[minX16584,minY16584,maxX16584,maxY16584]:null;
  }

  function earthlineBBoxIntersects16584(a16584,b16584){

      a16584[3]>=b16584[1]&&a16584[1]<=b16584[3]);
  }
  function earthlinePolygonParts16584(g16584){

    if(g16584.type==="Polygon")return [g16584.coordinates];
    if(g16584.type==="MultiPolygon")return Array.isArray(g16584.coordinates)?g16584.coordinates:[];
    return [];
  }
  function earthlineRingBBox16584(ring16584){
    let minX16584=Infinity,minY16584=Infinity,maxX16584=-Infinity,maxY16584=-Infinity;
    for(const p16584 of (ring16584||[])){
      const x16584=Number(p16584&&p16584[0]),y16584=Number(p16584&&p16584[1]);
      if(!Number.isFinite(x16584)||!Number.isFinite(y16584))continue;
      minX16584=Math.min(minX16584,x16584);minY16584=Math.min(minY16584,y16584);
      maxX16584=Math.max(maxX16584,x16584);maxY16584=Math.max(maxY16584,y16584);
    }
    return [minX16584,minY16584,maxX16584,maxY16584].every(Number.isFinite)
      ?[minX16584,minY16584,maxX16584,maxY16584]:null;
  }
  function earthlinePointInRing16584(x16584,y16584,ring16584){
    if(!Array.isArray(ring16584)||ring16584.length<3)return false;
    let inside16584=false;
    for(let i16584=0,j16584=ring16584.length-1;i16584<ring16584.length;j16584=i16584++){
      const a16584=ring16584[i16584]||[],b16584=ring16584[j16584]||[];
      const xi16584=Number(a16584[0]),yi16584=Number(a16584[1]),
            xj16584=Number(b16584[0]),yj16584=Number(b16584[1]);
      if(![xi16584,yi16584,xj16584,yj16584].every(Number.isFinite))continue;
      if(((yi16584>y16584)!==(yj16584>y16584))&&
         (x16584<(xj16584-xi16584)*(y16584-yi16584)/((yj16584-yi16584)||1e-15)+xi16584))
        inside16584=!inside16584;
    }
    return inside16584;
  }
  function earthlinePointInPolygon16584(x16584,y16584,rings16584){
    if(!Array.isArray(rings16584)||!rings16584.length||
       !earthlinePointInRing16584(x16584,y16584,rings16584[0]))return false;
    for(let i16584=1;i16584<rings16584.length;i16584++)
      if(earthlinePointInRing16584(x16584,y16584,rings16584[i16584]))return false;
    return true;
  }
  function earthlineExtractAreaEvidence16584(fc16584,bounds16584){
    const features16584=[],parts16584=[];
    for(const f16584 of (fc16584&&fc16584.features||[])){
      const fb16584=earthlineFeatureBBox16584(f16584);
      if(fb16584&&!earthlineBBoxIntersects16584(fb16584,bounds16584))continue;
      const polygons16584=earthlinePolygonParts16584(f16584.geometry);
      if(!polygons16584.length)continue;
      let kept16584=0;
      for(const rings16584 of polygons16584){
        const rb16584=earthlineRingBBox16584(rings16584&&rings16584[0]);
        if(rb16584&&earthlineBBoxIntersects16584(rb16584,bounds16584)){
          parts16584.push({rings:rings16584,bbox:rb16584,properties:f16584.properties||{}});
          kept16584++;
        }
      }
      if(kept16584)features16584.push(f16584);
    }
    return {features:features16584,parts:parts16584};
  }

  function earthlinePointInRing16584(x16584,y16584,ring16584){

      if(earthlinePointInRing16584(x16584,y16584,rings16584[i16584]))return false;
    return true;
  }
  function earthlineExtractAreaEvidence16584(fc16584,bounds16584){
    const features16584=[],parts16584=[];
    for(const f16584 of (fc16584&&fc16584.features||[])){
      const fb16584=earthlineFeatureBBox16584(f16584);
      if(fb16584&&!earthlineBBoxIntersects16584(fb16584,bounds16584))continue;
      const polygons16584=earthlinePolygonParts16584(f16584.geometry);
      if(!polygons16584.length)continue;
      let kept16584=0;
      for(const rings16584 of polygons16584){
        const rb16584=earthlineRingBBox16584(rings16584&&rings16584[0]);
        if(rb16584&&earthlineBBoxIntersects16584(rb16584,bounds16584)){
          parts16584.push({rings:rings16584,bbox:rb16584,properties:f16584.properties||{}});
          kept16584++;
        }
      }
      if(kept16584)features16584.push(f16584);
    }
    return {features:features16584,parts:parts16584};
  }
  async function earthlineResolveLandValidity16584(bounds16584,runToken16584){
    const started16584=performance.now();
    const product16584={
      build:"EARTHLINE 16584",
      product:"landValidity",
      sourceTier:"open-data",
      acquisitionResult:"unavailable",
      governedExtent:bounds16584.slice(),
      sourceVintage:"Natural Earth final v5.0.0 GeoJSON · repository commit 2021-12-08",
      sourceCommit:"ace5fed0eaf3c6c03c951e75b439ba8fffbc218e",
      landSurfaceGeometry:null,inlandWaterGeometry:null,
      at:new Date().toISOString()
    };
    const [landFetch16584,lakeFetch16584]=await Promise.all([
      earthlineNE50Fetch16584("land"),earthlineNE50Fetch16584("lakes")
    ]);
    if(!isCurrentRun(runToken16584))return Object.assign(product16584,{failureReason:"superseded-run"});
    product16584.landSurfaceGeometry={
      sourceTier:"open-data",source:landFetch16584.url||EARTHLINE_NE50_LAND_16584,
      sourceVintage:product16584.sourceVintage,

      downloadMs:Number(landFetch16584.elapsedMs||0),error:landFetch16584.error||null
    };
    product16584.inlandWaterGeometry={
      sourceTier:"open-data",source:lakeFetch16584.url||EARTHLINE_NE50_LAKES_16584,
      sourceVintage:product16584.sourceVintage,

      downloadMs:Number(lakeFetch16584.elapsedMs||0),error:lakeFetch16584.error||null

      product16584.failureReason=[
        !landFetch16584.ok?"land "+String(landFetch16584.error||"failed"):null,
        !lakeFetch16584.ok?"lakes "+String(lakeFetch16584.error||"failed"):null

      window.EARTHLINE_LAND_VALIDITY_16584=product16584;
      return product16584;
    }
    const landEvidence16584=earthlineExtractAreaEvidence16584(landFetch16584.data,bounds16584);
    const waterEvidence16584=earthlineExtractAreaEvidence16584(lakeFetch16584.data,bounds16584);
    product16584.landSurfaceGeometry.mappedFeatureCount=landEvidence16584.features.length;
    product16584.landSurfaceGeometry.mappedAreaCount=landEvidence16584.features.length;
    product16584.landSurfaceGeometry.mappedPolygonPartCount=landEvidence16584.parts.length;
    product16584.inlandWaterGeometry.mappedFeatureCount=waterEvidence16584.features.length;
    product16584.inlandWaterGeometry.mappedAreaCount=waterEvidence16584.features.length;

      .filter(Boolean).slice(0,12);

  function earthlineLandValidityMask16584(dem16584,product16584){
    const w16584=dem16584.w,h16584=dem16584.h,b16584=dem16584.bounds;
    const valid16584=new Uint8Array(w16584*h16584),outsideLandMask16632=new Uint8Array(w16584*h16584),inlandWaterMask16632=new Uint8Array(w16584*h16584);
    const landParts16584=Array.isArray(product16584&&product16584.landParts)?product16584.landParts:[];
    const waterParts16584=Array.isArray(product16584&&product16584.waterParts)?product16584.waterParts:[];
    let outsideLand16584=0,inlandWater16584=0,validCount16584=0;
    for(let y16584=0;y16584<h16584;y16584++){
      const lat16584=b16584[3]-(b16584[3]-b16584[1])*(y16584/(h16584-1));
      for(let x16584=0;x16584<w16584;x16584++){
        const lng16584=b16584[0]+(b16584[2]-b16584[0])*(x16584/(w16584-1));
        let onLand16584=false;
        for(const part16584 of landParts16584){
          if(lng16584<part16584.bbox[0]||lng16584>part16584.bbox[2]||
             lat16584<part16584.bbox[1]||lat16584>part16584.bbox[3])continue;
          if(earthlinePointInPolygon16584(lng16584,lat16584,part16584.rings)){onLand16584=true;break;}
        }
        if(!onLand16584){outsideLand16584++;outsideLandMask16632[y16584*w16584+x16584]=1;continue;}
        let inWater16584=false;
        for(const part16584 of waterParts16584){
          if(lng16584<part16584.bbox[0]||lng16584>part16584.bbox[2]||
             lat16584<part16584.bbox[1]||lat16584>part16584.bbox[3])continue;
          if(earthlinePointInPolygon16584(lng16584,lat16584,part16584.rings)){inWater16584=true;break;}
        }
        if(inWater16584){inlandWater16584++;inlandWaterMask16632[y16584*w16584+x16584]=1;continue;}
        valid16584[y16584*w16584+x16584]=1;validCount16584++;
      }
    }
    const audit16584={
      build:"EARTHLINE 16584",
      grid:{w:w16584,h:h16584,total:w16584*h16584,rule:"DEM node coordinate inside land AND outside Natural Earth lake polygon"},
      mappedFeatureCount:{
        land:Number(product16584&&product16584.landSurfaceGeometry&&product16584.landSurfaceGeometry.mappedFeatureCount||0),
        inlandWater:Number(product16584&&product16584.inlandWaterGeometry&&product16584.inlandWaterGeometry.mappedFeatureCount||0)
      },
      mappedAreaCount:{
        land:Number(product16584&&product16584.landSurfaceGeometry&&product16584.landSurfaceGeometry.mappedAreaCount||0),
        inlandWater:Number(product16584&&product16584.inlandWaterGeometry&&product16584.inlandWaterGeometry.mappedAreaCount||0)
      },
      rasterizedInvalidWaterCellCount:outsideLand16584+inlandWater16584,
      outsideLandCellCount:outsideLand16584,
      inlandWaterCellCount:inlandWater16584,
      validLandCellCount:validCount16584,
      at:new Date().toISOString()
    };
    window.EARTHLINE_LAND_VALIDITY_GRID_AUDIT_16584=audit16584;
    return {mask:valid16584,audit:audit16584,outsideLandMask16632,inlandWaterMask16632};
  }
  function earthlineLandValidityStatus16584(){
    const p16584=window.EARTHLINE_LAND_VALIDITY_16584||null,a16584=window.EARTHLINE_LAND_VALIDITY_GRID_AUDIT_16584||null;
    if(!p16584)return " · LAND VALIDITY pending";
    if(p16584.acquisitionResult!=="features-found")
      return " · LAND VALIDITY unavailable: "+String(p16584.failureReason||"source unavailable");
    if(!a16584)return " · LAND VALIDITY geometry acquired";
    return " · LAND VALIDITY "+Number(a16584.mappedFeatureCount&&a16584.mappedFeatureCount.land||0)+" land / "+
      Number(a16584.mappedFeatureCount&&a16584.mappedFeatureCount.inlandWater||0)+" water features / "+
      Number(a16584.rasterizedInvalidWaterCellCount||0)+" invalid cells ("+
      Number(a16584.outsideLandCellCount||0)+" ocean/coast + "+Number(a16584.inlandWaterCellCount||0)+" inland)";
  }

  function earthlineLandValidityMask16584(dem16584,product16584){
    const w16584=dem16584.w,h16584=dem16584.h,b16584=dem16584.bounds;
    const valid16584=new Uint8Array(w16584*h16584),outsideLandMask16632=new Uint8Array(w16584*h16584),inlandWaterMask16632=new Uint8Array(w16584*h16584);
    const landParts16584=Array.isArray(product16584&&product16584.landParts)?product16584.landParts:[];
    const waterParts16584=Array.isArray(product16584&&product16584.waterParts)?product16584.waterParts:[];
    let outsideLand16584=0,inlandWater16584=0,validCount16584=0;
    for(let y16584=0;y16584<h16584;y16584++){
      const lat16584=b16584[3]-(b16584[3]-b16584[1])*(y16584/(h16584-1));
      for(let x16584=0;x16584<w16584;x16584++){
        const lng16584=b16584[0]+(b16584[2]-b16584[0])*(x16584/(w16584-1));
        let onLand16584=false;
        for(const part16584 of landParts16584){
          if(lng16584<part16584.bbox[0]||lng16584>part16584.bbox[2]||
             lat16584<part16584.bbox[1]||lat16584>part16584.bbox[3])continue;
          if(earthlinePointInPolygon16584(lng16584,lat16584,part16584.rings)){onLand16584=true;break;}
        }
        if(!onLand16584){outsideLand16584++;outsideLandMask16632[y16584*w16584+x16584]=1;continue;}
        let inWater16584=false;
        for(const part16584 of waterParts16584){
          if(lng16584<part16584.bbox[0]||lng16584>part16584.bbox[2]||
             lat16584<part16584.bbox[1]||lat16584>part16584.bbox[3])continue;
          if(earthlinePointInPolygon16584(lng16584,lat16584,part16584.rings)){inWater16584=true;break;}
        }
        if(inWater16584){inlandWater16584++;inlandWaterMask16632[y16584*w16584+x16584]=1;continue;}
        valid16584[y16584*w16584+x16584]=1;validCount16584++;
      }
    }
    const audit16584={
      build:"EARTHLINE 16584",
      grid:{w:w16584,h:h16584,total:w16584*h16584,rule:"DEM node coordinate inside land AND outside Natural Earth lake polygon"},
      mappedFeatureCount:{
        land:Number(product16584&&product16584.landSurfaceGeometry&&product16584.landSurfaceGeometry.mappedFeatureCount||0),
        inlandWater:Number(product16584&&product16584.inlandWaterGeometry&&product16584.inlandWaterGeometry.mappedFeatureCount||0)
      },
      mappedAreaCount:{
        land:Number(product16584&&product16584.landSurfaceGeometry&&product16584.landSurfaceGeometry.mappedAreaCount||0),
        inlandWater:Number(product16584&&product16584.inlandWaterGeometry&&product16584.inlandWaterGeometry.mappedAreaCount||0)
      },
      rasterizedInvalidWaterCellCount:outsideLand16584+inlandWater16584,
      outsideLandCellCount:outsideLand16584,
      inlandWaterCellCount:inlandWater16584,
      validLandCellCount:validCount16584,
      at:new Date().toISOString()
    };
    window.EARTHLINE_LAND_VALIDITY_GRID_AUDIT_16584=audit16584;
    return {mask:valid16584,audit:audit16584,outsideLandMask16632,inlandWaterMask16632};
  }
  function earthlineLandValidityStatus16584(){
    const p16584=window.EARTHLINE_LAND_VALIDITY_16584||null,a16584=window.EARTHLINE_LAND_VALIDITY_GRID_AUDIT_16584||null;
    if(!p16584)return " · LAND VALIDITY pending";

      return " · LAND VALIDITY unavailable: "+String(p16584.failureReason||"source unavailable");
    if(!a16584)return " · LAND VALIDITY geometry acquired";
    return " · LAND VALIDITY "+Number(a16584.mappedFeatureCount&&a16584.mappedFeatureCount.land||0)+" land / "+
      Number(a16584.mappedFeatureCount&&a16584.mappedFeatureCount.inlandWater||0)+" water features / "+
      Number(a16584.rasterizedInvalidWaterCellCount||0)+" invalid cells ("+
      Number(a16584.outsideLandCellCount||0)+" ocean/coast + "+Number(a16584.inlandWaterCellCount||0)+" inland)";
  }

  async function runPropertyDirect(q){
    let task;
    /* EARTHLINE 16480 — authoritative Property run ownership.
       The live Regional controller captured legacy run15786 at install time and
       continued routing explicit Property targets through that older queue even
       after terminalPropertyRun became the governed Property lifecycle owner.
       When an explicit current Property target exists, call that governed owner
       directly. New/unresolved searches retain the existing resolver/legacy path. */

      target16480&&

      typeof governed16480==='function'
    );
    if(explicitTarget16480)task=governed16480();
    else if(!q&&typeof window.earthlineSetCurrentViewAsAnalysis15827==='function')task=window.earthlineSetCurrentViewAsAnalysis15827();
    else if(typeof propertyRun==='function')task=propertyRun();
    else if(typeof runCore15786==='function')task=runCore15786();
    else throw new Error('property analysis engine unavailable');
    try{
      return await withTimeout(task,55000,'property analysis');
    }catch(err){
      /* 16172: cancel cooperative terrain/hydrology loops so a timed-out property
         run cannot publish late into a newer focus/regional result. */
      const st=state();if(st&&Number.isFinite(Number(st.searchGen)))st.searchGen++;
      const raw=String(err&&err.message||err||'property analysis failed');
      if(/timed out|exceeded/i.test(raw)){
        const stage=String(st&&st.currentStage||window.EARTHLINE_PROPERTY_ACTIVE_STAGE_16178&&window.EARTHLINE_PROPERTY_ACTIVE_STAGE_16178.stage||'unknown stage');
        const timings=st&&st.lastTiming&&typeof st.lastTiming==='object'?JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(st.lastTiming)):{};
        window.EARTHLINE_PROPERTY_TIMEOUT_AUDIT_16178={build:'EARTHLINE 16178',stage,timings,error:raw,at:new Date().toISOString()};
        const named=stage?stage.replace(/[-_]+/g,' '):'unknown stage';
        throw new Error('property analysis timed out during '+named+'. Recorded stage timings: '+JSON.stringify(timings));
      }
      throw err;
    }
  }

  function opacityForZoom(z,start=10){const floor=Math.max(10,Number(start)||10);if(z<=floor)return 1;if(z>=floor+3)return 0;return Math.max(.28,1-((z-floor)/3)*.72)}
  function regionalVisibilityFloor16337(){
    const d=displayed(),pub=window.EARTHLINE_REGIONAL_PUBLICATION_VIEW_16337||null;
    const currentToken=String(d&&d.runToken||document.documentElement.dataset.earthlineActiveRun||'');
    return pub&&currentToken&&String(pub.runToken||'')===currentToken&&Number.isFinite(Number(pub.zoom))
      ?Math.max(10,Number(pub.zoom)):10;
  }

  function applyHandoff(){
    const m=map();if(!m)return false;
    const z=Number(m.getZoom?.()||0),regional=isRegionalDisplayed(),p=prompt();
    let analysisFactor=1,contextFactor=1,stage='none';
    const propertyRunActive=document.documentElement.dataset.earthlinePropertyRunState==='running'||document.documentElement.classList.contains('earthline-property-running-16233');
    const currentTier=propertyRunActive?'property':(tier()||'regional');
    const visibilityFloor16337=regionalVisibilityFloor16337();
    const crossedIntoRegionalZoom=currentTier==='regional'&&Number.isFinite(lastObservedZoom16165)&&lastObservedZoom16165>10&&z<=10;
    if(crossedIntoRegionalZoom)clearCorridorTransientState('regional-zoom-threshold-crossing',true);
    lastObservedZoom16165=z;
    if(currentTier==='focus'){analysisFactor=1;contextFactor=0;stage='none'}
    else if(currentTier==='property'){analysisFactor=0;contextFactor=0;stage='none'}
    else if(regional&&!document.documentElement.classList.contains('earthline-direct-property-handoff-16250')&&!(window.EARTHLINE_PROPERTY_TARGET_16201&&window.EARTHLINE_PROPERTY_TARGET_16201.code)&&z>visibilityFloor16337){
      analysisFactor=opacityForZoom(z,visibilityFloor16337);
      contextFactor=analysisFactor;
      stage=z>=visibilityFloor16337+3?'required':'offer'
    }
    document.documentElement.dataset.earthlineAnalysisTier=currentTier;
    const svg=document.getElementById(REGIONAL_OVERLAY);
    if(svg){svg.style.opacity=String(analysisFactor);svg.dataset.analysisTier=currentTier;svg.style.pointerEvents='none'}
    setLayerOpacity(AQUIFER_FILL,'fill-opacity',.40*contextFactor);
    setLayerOpacity(AQUIFER_LINE,'line-opacity',.94*contextFactor);
    if(stage==='none'){p.classList.remove('open');p.dataset.stage='none'}
    else{
      p.classList.add('open');p.dataset.stage=stage;
      const body=p.querySelector('p');
      if(body)body.textContent=stage==='required'
        ?'The regional layers have reached their valid display limit. Declare the map-center location to run a fixed 3 km focus analysis.'
        :'Regional layers are fading as you zoom. Declare this location to create a reproducible, viewport-independent focus analysis.';
      const btn=p.querySelector('button');
      if(btn){btn.textContent=focusBusy?'ANALYZING FIXED LOCATION…':'ANALYZE THIS LOCATION';btn.disabled=focusBusy}
    }
    if(stage!==lastStage){
      lastStage=stage;
      window.EARTHLINE_FOCUS_HANDOFF_STATE_16160=window.EARTHLINE_FOCUS_HANDOFF_STATE_16159={
        build:BUILD,stage,zoom:z,tier:currentTier,analysisFactor,contextFactor,at:new Date().toISOString()
      }
    }
    window.EARTHLINE_FOCUS_VISIBILITY_AUDIT_16161=window.EARTHLINE_FOCUS_VISIBILITY_AUDIT_16160={
      build:BUILD,tier:currentTier,zoom:z,analysisFactor,contextFactor,
      regionalVisibilityFloor16337:visibilityFloor16337,
      regionalPublicationView16337:window.EARTHLINE_REGIONAL_PUBLICATION_VIEW_16337||null,
      overlayVisible:analysisFactor>0,regionalContextVisible:contextFactor>0,at:new Date().toISOString()
    };
    updateBreadcrumb();return true
  }

  function nativeWater16350(){
    const map=m(),ids=['el-live-flow-casing-15970','el-live-flow-line-15970','el-live-flow-arrow-15970'];

      let exists=false,visibility=null;
      try{exists=!!(map&&map.getLayer&&map.getLayer(id));if(exists){map.setLayoutProperty(id,'visibility','visible');visibility=map.getLayoutProperty(id,'visibility')||'visible';}}catch(_){exists=false;}
      layers[id]={exists,visibility};ready=ready&&exists&&visibility!=='none';
    }
    return {owner:'native-mapbox',ready,layers};
  }

          t.textContent=label;g.appendChild(t);lg.appendChild(g);gradeLabels.push(label);
        }
      }
      swales++;
    }
    /* EARTHLINE 16350 — Regional water is counted here for audit only; SVG does not render it.
       Directional presentation is owned by the native Mapbox flow line + line-placement arrow layers. */
    for(const f of (lastData.flows&&lastData.flows.features||[])){
      if(!f||!f.properties)continue;
      if(f.properties.feature_type==='flow'&&f.geometry&&f.geometry.type==='LineString')waterPaths++;
      if(f.properties.feature_type==='flow-arrow')arrows++;
    }
    setTimeout(()=>{try{window.earthlineDecorateSwales16149&&window.earthlineDecorateSwales16149();}catch(_){}},0);
    hideNative();
    const nativeWater=nativeWater16350();
    window.EARTHLINE_REGIONAL_WATER_OWNERSHIP_AUDIT_16350={build:'EARTHLINE 16350',owner:'native-mapbox',svgWaterRendered:false,nativeWater,waterPathFeatures:waterPaths,flowArrowFeatures:arrows,renderedAt:new Date().toISOString()};
    window.EARTHLINE_REGIONAL_DISPLAY_AUDIT_16040=window.EARTHLINE_REGIONAL_DISPLAY_AUDIT_16030=window.EARTHLINE_REGIONAL_DISPLAY_AUDIT_16020={build:'EARTHLINE-REGIONAL-FINAL-16050',contourLines,elevationLabels:labels,waterPaths,directionArrows:arrows,waterOwner:'native-mapbox',svgWaterRendered:false,nativeWaterReady:!!nativeWater.ready,swaleLines:swales,swaleGradeCounts:gradeCounts,swaleGradeLabels:gradeLabels.length,topSwaleGradeLabel:gradeLabels[0]||'',buttonText:(document.getElementById('earthlineMapRun16020')||{}).textContent||'',renderedAt:new Date().toISOString()};return labels>0&&waterPaths>0&&nativeWater.ready&&swales>0&&gradeCounts.A>0&&gradeLabels.length>0;
  }
  function schedule(){
    if(activeTier16168()==="property")return;
    cancelAnimationFrame(scheduled);scheduled=requestAnimationFrame(()=>render(lastData));
  }
  function clean(){for(const id of ['earthline15857strip','earthline15861strip','earthlineMapRun15970','earthlineMapRun16020','runBtnSide'])document.getElementById(id)?.remove();}
  function installButton(){clean();return null;}
  function install(){if(installed)return;installed=true;clean();const map=m();if(map&&map.on){map.on('moveend',schedule);map.on('zoomend',schedule);map.on('rotateend',schedule);map.on('pitchend',schedule);map.on('resize',schedule);map.on('style.load',()=>{if(lastData)setTimeout(schedule,80);});map.on('styledata',()=>{});}window.addEventListener('resize',schedule,{passive:true});setInterval(clean,350);window.earthlineRenderRegionalOverlay16020=render;window.earthlineRegionalDisplayAudit16040=()=>window.EARTHLINE_REGIONAL_DISPLAY_AUDIT_16040||window.EARTHLINE_REGIONAL_DISPLAY_AUDIT_16030||window.EARTHLINE_REGIONAL_DISPLAY_AUDIT_16020||{build:BUILD,rendered:false,buttonText:(document.getElementById('earthlineMapRun16020')||{}).textContent||''};if(window.EARTHLINE_REGIONAL_VISUAL_DATA_16020)render(window.EARTHLINE_REGIONAL_VISUAL_DATA_16020);document.documentElement.dataset.earthlineRegionalFinal='16050';}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',install,{once:true});else install();
})();
</script>
